   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: import java.time.Instant;
   8: import java.util.ArrayList;
   9: import java.util.Comparator;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.Optional;
  12: import java.util.HashSet;
  13: import java.util.Set;
  14: import java.util.function.Consumer;
  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */

 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))

 114:                 .limit(limit)

 835:                                     + (closeReason == null || closeReason.isBlank() ? "" : " via " + closeReason)
 836:                                     + "; pre-exit opportunity evidence is consumed and cannot justify a new position.");

 839:                 });

 862:         return assessEntryQuality(current, authoritativeAtr, current == null ? null : current.getLatestPrice());

 865:     private EntryQuality assessEntryQuality(TradeSignal current, BigDecimal authoritativeAtr, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
 866:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null || executionPrice == null
 867:                 || executionPrice.signum() <= 0) {
 868:             return new EntryQuality(0, "UNKNOWN", 0d, 0d, 0d, 0L, false, null, 0, 0d, false, null);
 869:         }
 870: 
 871:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EVIDENCE_WINDOW);
 872:         List<TradeSignal> recent = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt());
 873: 
 874:         BigDecimal reference = executionPrice;
 875:         BigDecimal recentHigh = executionPrice;
 876:         Instant recentHighAt = current.getGeneratedAt();
 877:         for (TradeSignal s : recent) {
 878:             if (s.getGeneratedAt() == null || s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())) continue;
 879:             if (s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) break;
 880:             if (s.getLatestPrice() != null && s.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0
 881:                     && s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(reference) < 0) {
 882:                 reference = s.getLatestPrice();
 883:             }
 884:             // FIX-091 / Fix 7: mirror the existing rolling-low memory with a rolling high.
 885:             // We intentionally use already-persisted signal prices so Replay and Production
 886:             // observe the same pre-wallet evidence and no new market-data dependency is added.
 887:             if (s.getLatestPrice() != null && s.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0
 888:                     && s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(recentHigh) > 0) {
 889:                 recentHigh = s.getLatestPrice();
 890:                 recentHighAt = s.getGeneratedAt();
 891:             }
 892:         }
 893: 
 894:         // FIX-055 + FIX-105: keep the opportunity anchor/best floor only while the opportunity
 895:         // is fresh. currentOpportunity() is Replay-aware, and age is measured exclusively from
 896:         // persisted/replayed candle-time evidence (startedAt -> signal.generatedAt), never wall-clock
 897:         // time. Therefore Production and Replay execute this exact same branch deterministically.
 898:         java.util.Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> activeOpportunity = currentOpportunity(
 899:                 current.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"));
 900:         String chaseReferenceSource = "ROLLING_30M";
 901:         if (activeOpportunity.isPresent()) {
 902:             ExecutionOpportunity opp = activeOpportunity.get();
 903:             boolean anchorStillFresh = false;
 904:             long opportunityAgeMinutesForReference = 0L;
 905: 
 906:             if (opp.getStartedAt() != null) {
 907:                 Duration opportunityAge = Duration.between(opp.getStartedAt(), current.getGeneratedAt());
 908:                 opportunityAgeMinutesForReference = Math.max(0L, opportunityAge.toMinutes());
 909:                 // FIX-105 boundary is inclusive: exactly 120 minutes keeps FIX-055 protection;
 910:                 // the first instant beyond two hours returns authority to the rolling 30m reference.
 911:                 anchorStillFresh = !opportunityAge.isNegative()
 912:                         && opportunityAge.compareTo(ANCHOR_MAX_AGE) <= 0;
 913:             }
 914: 
 915:             if (anchorStillFresh) {
 916:                 BigDecimal anchor = opp.getAnchorEntryPrice();
 917:                 BigDecimal best = opp.getBestEntryPrice();
 918:                 if (anchor != null && anchor.signum() > 0 && anchor.compareTo(reference) < 0) {
 919:                     reference = anchor;
 920:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_ANCHOR";
 921:                 }
 922:                 if (best != null && best.signum() > 0 && best.compareTo(reference) < 0) {
 923:                     reference = best;
 924:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_BEST";
 925:                 }
 926:             }
 927: 
 928:             // FIX-105 diagnostics are deliberately log-only in v1: no schema expansion is required
 929:             // to prove which reference won in Production or Replay. Persisted decision/opportunity
 930:             // evidence remains unchanged.
 931:             log.debug("FIX-105 chase reference symbol={} source={} reference={} opportunityAgeMinutes={} anchorFresh={}",
 932:                     current.getSymbol(), chaseReferenceSource, reference, opportunityAgeMinutesForReference, anchorStillFresh);
 933:         }
 934: 
 935:         double expansionPercent = executionPrice.subtract(reference)
 936:                 .divide(reference, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 937:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).doubleValue();
 938: 
 939:         double atrExtension = 0d;
 940:         BigDecimal atrForExtension = authoritativeAtr != null && authoritativeAtr.signum() > 0
 941:                 ? authoritativeAtr
 942:                 : current.getAtrAtSignal();
 943:         if (atrForExtension != null && atrForExtension.signum() > 0) {
 944:             atrExtension = executionPrice.subtract(reference).max(BigDecimal.ZERO)
 945:                     .divide(atrForExtension, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
 946:         }
 947: 
 948:         long ageMinutes = activeOpportunity
 949:                 .map(o -> o.getStartedAt() == null ? 0L
 950:                         : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(o.getStartedAt(), current.getGeneratedAt()).toMinutes()))
 951:                 .orElse(0L);
 952: 
 953:         double rr = currentRewardRisk(current, executionPrice);
 954: 
 955:         // FIX-091 / Fix 7: a prior high is considered a recent rejection only when price
 956:         // subsequently backed away and the current entry is returning close to that same zone.
 957:         // v1 is deliberately conservative: this only reduces Entry Quality; it never hard-vetoes.
 958:         int rejectionCount = 0;
 959:         double distanceFromRejectedHighPercent = 0d;
 960:         BigDecimal recentRejectedHigh = null;
 961:         if (recentHigh != null && recentHigh.signum() > 0 && recentHighAt != null
 962:                 && recentHighAt.isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())) {
 963:             for (TradeSignal s : recent) {
 964:                 if (s.getGeneratedAt() == null || !s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(recentHighAt)
 965:                         || s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt()) || s.getLatestPrice() == null) continue;
 966:                 if (s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(recentHigh) < 0) rejectionCount++;
 967:             }
 968:             distanceFromRejectedHighPercent = recentHigh.subtract(executionPrice).abs()
 969:                     .divide(recentHigh, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 970:                     .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).doubleValue();

2534:     private void saveOpportunity(TradeSignal signal, Evidence evidence, String status, String source,
2535:                                  int positionPercent, String code, String explanation) {
2536:         Instant now = Instant.now();
2537:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity = currentOpportunity(signal.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
2538:                 .orElseGet(() -> ExecutionOpportunity.builder()
2539:                         .symbol(signal.getSymbol())
2540:                         .direction("BUY")
2541:                         .startedAt(signal.getGeneratedAt())
2542:                         .createdAt(now)
2543:                         .build());
2544: 
2545:         opportunity.setStatus(normalizeStatus(status));
2546:         opportunity.setLastEvidenceAt(signal.getGeneratedAt());
2547:         opportunity.setLatestSignal(signal);
2548: 
2549:         // FIX-055: keep the opportunity's actual price history independent of the
2550:         // recent-signal lookup window. For a BUY opportunity, the first valid price
2551:         // is the anchor and the lowest valid observed price is the best entry reference.
2552:         if (signal.getLatestPrice() != null && signal.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0) {
2553:             if (opportunity.getAnchorEntryPrice() == null || opportunity.getAnchorEntryPrice().signum() <= 0) {
2554:                 opportunity.setAnchorEntryPrice(signal.getLatestPrice());
2555:             }
2556:             if (opportunity.getBestEntryPrice() == null || opportunity.getBestEntryPrice().signum() <= 0
2557:                     || signal.getLatestPrice().compareTo(opportunity.getBestEntryPrice()) < 0) {
2558:                 opportunity.setBestEntryPrice(signal.getLatestPrice());
2559:             }
2560:         }
2561:         // FIX-091 / Fix 8: update or reset the opportunity peak using the current signal.
2562:         // The peak expires after 15 minutes so an old BUY cannot permanently poison a newly
2563:         // rebuilt setup. A genuinely stronger current signal establishes a fresh peak.
2564:         updateOpportunityPeak(opportunity, signal);
2565: 
2566:         opportunity.setEvidenceCount(evidence.observationCount());
2567:         opportunity.setBuyCount(evidence.buyCount());
2568:         opportunity.setWatchCount(evidence.watchCount());
2569:         opportunity.setNeutralCount(evidence.neutralCount());
2570:         opportunity.setBearishCount(evidence.bearishCount());
2571:         opportunity.setEvidenceScore(evidence.evidenceScore());
2572:         opportunity.setOpportunityHealth(evidence.opportunityHealth());
2573:         opportunity.setHealthMomentum(evidence.healthMomentum());
2574:         opportunity.setEvidenceMomentum(evidence.evidenceMomentum());
2575:         opportunity.setLastBearishAt(evidence.lastBearishAt());
2576:         opportunity.setAverageSignalScore(evidence.averageScore());
2577:         opportunity.setAverageConfidence(evidence.averageConfidence());
2578:         opportunity.setFiveMinuteDecision(evidence.fiveMinute() == null ? null : evidence.fiveMinute().name());
2579:         opportunity.setOneHourDecision(evidence.oneHour() == null ? null : evidence.oneHour().name());
2580:         opportunity.setExecutionSource(source);
2581:         opportunity.setRecommendedPositionPercent(positionPercent);
2582:         opportunity.setDecisionCode(code);
2583:         opportunity.setDecisionExplanation(explanation);
2584:         opportunity.setUpdatedAt(now);
2585:         saveOpportunityEntity(opportunity);
2586:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java lines 1-160 =====
   1: package com.crypto.wallet.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
   4: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: 
   8: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   9: import java.math.MathContext;
  10: import java.util.Locale;
  11: 
  12: /**
  13:  * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
  14:  *
  15:  * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
  16:  * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
  17:  * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
  18:  * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
  19:  *
  20:  * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
  21:  * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
  22:  */
  23: @Component
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: @Slf4j
  26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
  27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  28: 
  29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
  30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
  31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
  32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
  33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
  34:         }
  35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
  37:         BigDecimal minimum;
  38:         try {
  39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
  40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
  42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
  43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
  44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
  45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
  46:         }
  47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
  48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
  49:         }
  50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
  54:     }
  55: }

 108:     /**
 109:      * FIX-112C historical replay path. Replay reads persisted Production Order Book
 110:      * observations strictly as-of the historical evaluation timestamp and routes them
 111:      * through the same normalized evaluator used by live Production. Pre-V74 windows
 112:      * with no persisted evidence remain explicitly UNAVAILABLE.
 113:      */
 114:     public OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluateHistorical(
 115:             String symbol,
 116:             String interval,
 117:             SignalDecision currentDecision,
 118:             boolean entryAllowed,
 119:             BigDecimal currentPrice,
 120:             BigDecimal stopLoss,
 121:             BigDecimal takeProfit,
 122:             Instant evaluatedAt
 123:     ) {
 124:         IntervalPolicy policy = properties.policyFor(interval == null ? "5m" : interval);
 125:         Instant snapshotTime = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
 126:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
 127:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 128:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 129:                     false, false, 0, "Order-book liquidity analysis is disabled.", snapshotTime,
 130:                     policy, 0L);
 131:         }
 132: 
 133:         // FIX-112C: historical Replay reads only persisted Production observations
 134:         // whose observed_at is inside the exact as-of window. No live Binance book
 135:         // and no future observation can enter a historical decision.
 136:         Instant windowStart = snapshotTime.minusSeconds(policy.windowSeconds());
 137:         List<NormalizedObservation> observations = orderBookSnapshotService
 138:                 .find(symbol, windowStart, snapshotTime).stream()
 139:                 .map(row -> new NormalizedObservation(row.observedAt(),
 140:                         // FIX-112C parity preservation: an invalid persisted snapshot must remain
 141:                         // in the observation sequence. Pre-FIX-112 Production counted every snapshot
 142:                         // in the active window and, critically, returned UNAVAILABLE when the latest
 143:                         // snapshot itself had no valid midpoint. Dropping invalid rows here would
 144:                         // silently change that established Production behavior during Replay.
 145:                         row.bestBid() == null || row.bestAsk() == null
 146:                                 || row.bestBid().add(row.bestAsk()).signum() <= 0
 147:                                 ? null
 148:                                 : new SnapshotMetrics(
 149:                                         row.bidDepth(), row.askDepth(), row.depthImbalance(),
 150:                                         row.spreadBps() == null ? null : row.spreadBps().divide(ONE_HUNDRED, MC),
 151:                                         row.bidWallPrice(), row.bidWallQuantity(),
 152:                                         row.askWallPrice(), row.askWallQuantity())))
 153:                 .toList();
 154:         if (observations.isEmpty()) {
 155:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.UNAVAILABLE, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 156:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 157:                     false, false, 0,
 158:                     "No persisted Production order-book observation exists for this historical replay window; live data was not substituted.",
 159:                     snapshotTime, policy, 0L);
 160:         }
 161:         return evaluateNormalized(symbol, interval, currentDecision, entryAllowed,
 162:                 currentPrice, stopLoss, takeProfit, snapshotTime, policy, observations);
 163:     }
 164: 
 165:     public OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluate(
 166:             String symbol,
 167:             String interval,
 168:             SignalDecision currentDecision,
 169:             boolean entryAllowed,
 170:             BigDecimal currentPrice,
 171:             BigDecimal stopLoss,
 172:             BigDecimal takeProfit,
 173:             Instant evaluatedAt
 174:     ) {
 175:         IntervalPolicy policy = properties.policyFor(interval == null ? "5m" : interval);
 176:         Instant snapshotTime = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
 177:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
 178:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 179:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 180:                     false, false, 0, "Order-book liquidity analysis is disabled.", snapshotTime,
 181:                     policy, 0L);
 182:         }
 183: 
 184:         String normalizedSymbol = normalize(symbol);
 185:         Deque<OrderBookSnapshot> deque = historyBySymbol.get(normalizedSymbol);
 186:         if (deque == null || deque.isEmpty()) {
 187:             collectSafely(normalizedSymbol);
 188:             deque = historyBySymbol.get(normalizedSymbol);
 189:         }
 190:         if (deque == null || deque.isEmpty()) {
 191:             // FIX-091 / Fix 2: in LIVE analysis, zero observations are not proof that liquidity is safe.
 192:             // Hold a fresh entry until the configured sample minimum is reached. Historical replay uses
 193:             // evaluateHistorical(...) and deliberately keeps its separate UNAVAILABLE limitation.
 194:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 195:                     false, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 196:                     false, false, 0, "Live order-book sampling is not ready (0/" + policy.minimumObservations()
 197:                             + "). Fresh entry is held until enough observations are collected.", snapshotTime,
 198:                     policy, 0L);
 199:         }
 200: 
 201:         List<OrderBookSnapshot> snapshots;
 202:         synchronized (deque) {
 203:             Instant windowStart = snapshotTime.minusSeconds(policy.windowSeconds());
 204:             snapshots = deque.stream()
 205:                     .filter(snapshot -> !snapshot.capturedAt().isAfter(snapshotTime))
 206:                     .filter(snapshot -> !snapshot.capturedAt().isBefore(windowStart))
 207:                     .toList();
 208:         }
 209:         if (snapshots.isEmpty()) {
 210:             // FIX-091 / Fix 2: an enabled LIVE order-book service with no observation in the active
 211:             // window is an insufficient-sampling condition, not a neutral PASS.
 212:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 213:                     false, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 214:                     false, false, 0,
 215:                     "No live order-book observation exists inside the " + policy.windowSeconds()
 216:                             + " second window for interval " + interval + "; fresh entry is held until "
 217:                             + policy.minimumObservations() + " observations are available.",
 218:                     snapshotTime, policy, 0L);
 219:         }
 220: 
 221:         // FIX-112C parity preservation: keep every live snapshot in sequence and let the
 222:         // shared evaluator enforce the exact pre-refactor latest-snapshot validity gate.
 223:         // Do not filter individual invalid snapshots here; doing so changed live Production
 224:         // semantics by allowing an older valid snapshot to replace an invalid latest reading.
 225:         List<NormalizedObservation> observations = snapshots.stream()
 226:                 .map(snapshot -> {
 227:                     BigDecimal mid = midpoint(snapshot);
 228:                     return new NormalizedObservation(snapshot.capturedAt(),
 229:                             mid == null || mid.signum() <= 0 ? null : metrics(snapshot, mid));
 230:                 })
 231:                 .toList();
 232:         return evaluateNormalized(symbol, interval, currentDecision, entryAllowed,
 233:                 currentPrice, stopLoss, takeProfit, snapshotTime, policy, observations);
 234:     }
 235: 
 236:     /**
 237:      * FIX-112C shared decision path. Production normalizes live snapshots into this
 238:      * representation; Replay loads the same normalized observations from MySQL.
 239:      * All status/veto/wall-persistence logic below is therefore identical.
 240:      */

  52:     public DerivativesPositioningResult evaluate(String symbol, String interval, SignalDecision decision,
  53:                                                   boolean entryAllowed, Instant evaluatedAt) {
  54:         Instant now = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
  55:         if (!properties.enabled()) return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  56:                 null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
  57:                 "Derivatives positioning is disabled.", now);
  58:         try {
  59:             List<BinanceFundingRate> funding = client.fundingHistory(symbol, properties.fundingHistoryLimit());
  60:             List<BinanceOpenInterestPoint> oi = client.openInterestHistory(symbol, period(interval), properties.openInterestHistoryLimit());
  61:             if (funding.isEmpty() || oi.size() < 2) {
  62:                 return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.LEARNING, decision, entryAllowed,
  63:                         latestFunding(funding), percentile(funding), latestOi(oi), latestOiValue(oi), null,
  64:                         priceChange(symbol, interval), funding.size(), period(interval), -5,
  65:                         "Funding or open-interest history is still insufficient; no veto was applied.", now);
  66:             }
  67:             BigDecimal fundingRate = latestFunding(funding);
  68:             BigDecimal fundingPercentile = percentile(funding);
  69:             BigDecimal oiChange = percentChange(oi.get(oi.size() - 2).sumOpenInterest(), oi.get(oi.size() - 1).sumOpenInterest());
  70:             BigDecimal priceChange = priceChange(symbol, interval);
  71:             DerivativesPositioningStatus status = classify(fundingRate, oiChange, priceChange);
  72:             SignalDecision finalDecision = decision;
  73:             boolean allowed = entryAllowed;
  74:             int confidence = confidence(status);
  75:             if (isBullish(decision) && status == DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONGS_CROWDED
  76:                     && properties.vetoExtremeCrowding()
  77:                     && oiChange != null && oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.strongOpenInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0) {
  78:                 finalDecision = SignalDecision.WATCH;
  79:                 allowed = false;
  80:             }
  81:             if (isBearish(decision) && status == DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORTS_CROWDED
  82:                     && properties.vetoExtremeCrowding()
  83:                     && oiChange != null && oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.strongOpenInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0) {
  84:                 finalDecision = SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
  85:                 allowed = false;
  86:             }
  87:             String explanation = explanation(status, fundingRate, fundingPercentile, oiChange, priceChange, allowed);
  88:             BinanceOpenInterestPoint latest = oi.get(oi.size() - 1);
  89:             return result(status, finalDecision, allowed, fundingRate, fundingPercentile,
  90:                     latest.sumOpenInterest(), latest.sumOpenInterestValue(), oiChange, priceChange,
  91:                     funding.size(), period(interval), confidence, explanation, now);
  92:         } catch (RestClientResponseException ex) {
  93:             if (ex.getStatusCode().value() == 400) {
  94:                 return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  95:                         null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
  96:                         "No matching Binance USD-M perpetual contract is available for " + symbol + ".", now);
  97:             }
  98:             return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  99:                     null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), -10,
 100:                     "Binance futures positioning data is unavailable: " + ex.getMessage(), now);
 101:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 102:             return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
 103:                     null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), -10,
 104:                     "Binance futures positioning data is unavailable: " + ex.getMessage(), now);
 105:         }
 106:     }
 107: 
 108:     private DerivativesPositioningStatus classify(BigDecimal funding, BigDecimal oiChange, BigDecimal priceChange) {
 109:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.extremeFundingRate()) >= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONGS_CROWDED;
 110:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.extremeFundingRate().negate()) <= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORTS_CROWDED;
 111:         if (oiChange == null || priceChange == null) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.BALANCED;
 112:         boolean oiRising = oiChange.compareTo(properties.openInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0;
 113:         boolean oiFalling = oiChange.compareTo(properties.openInterestChangeThreshold().negate()) <= 0;
 114:         if (priceChange.signum() > 0 && oiRising) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.FRESH_LONG_BUILDUP;
 115:         if (priceChange.signum() < 0 && oiRising) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.FRESH_SHORT_BUILDUP;
 116:         if (priceChange.signum() > 0 && oiFalling) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORT_COVERING;
 117:         if (priceChange.signum() < 0 && oiFalling) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONG_LIQUIDATION;
 118:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.moderateFundingRate()) >= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.HEALTHY_BULLISH;
 119:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.moderateFundingRate().negate()) <= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.HEALTHY_BEARISH;
 120:         return oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.openInterestChangeThreshold()) < 0
 121:                 ? DerivativesPositioningStatus.LOW_CONVICTION : DerivativesPositioningStatus.BALANCED;
 122:     }
 123: 
 124:     private int confidence(DerivativesPositioningStatus status) {
 125:         return switch (status) {
 126:             case FRESH_LONG_BUILDUP, FRESH_SHORT_BUILDUP -> 8;
 127:             case HEALTHY_BULLISH, HEALTHY_BEARISH, BALANCED -> 3;
 128:             case SHORT_COVERING, LONG_LIQUIDATION, LOW_CONVICTION -> -5;
 129:             case LONGS_CROWDED, SHORTS_CROWDED -> -15;
 130:             case LEARNING -> -5;
 131:             case UNAVAILABLE -> -10;
 132:             case NOT_APPLICABLE -> 0;
 133:         };
 134:     }
 135: 
 136:     private String explanation(DerivativesPositioningStatus status, BigDecimal funding, BigDecimal percentile,
 137:                                BigDecimal oiChange, BigDecimal priceChange, boolean allowed) {
 138:         return "Positioning " + status + "; funding=" + value(funding) + ", percentile=" + value(percentile)
 139:                 + "%, OI change=" + value(oiChange) + "%, price change=" + value(priceChange)
 140:                 + "%. Entry " + (allowed ? "remains allowed" : "was blocked") + ".";
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     private BigDecimal priceChange(String symbol, String interval) {
 144:         List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandles(symbol, interval, PageRequest.of(0, 2)));
 145:         if (candles.size() < 2) return null;
 146:         candles.sort(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime));
 147:         return percentChange(candles.get(0).getClosePrice(), candles.get(1).getClosePrice());
 148:     }
 149: 
 150:     private BigDecimal latestFunding(List<BinanceFundingRate> rates) {
 151:         return rates.isEmpty() ? null : rates.get(rates.size() - 1).fundingRate();
 152:     }

 154:     private BigDecimal latestOiValue(List<BinanceOpenInterestPoint> points) { return points.isEmpty() ? null : points.get(points.size()-1).sumOpenInterestValue(); }

  45: ### Q1. Why did it exit on profit lock instead of take-profit? [PE]
  46: - **TP needed three ticks.** TP was 0.00000543373: entry 0.00000541 plus 3 A- the 1m ATR of 0.00000000791. Prices move in steps of 0.00000001, so reaching TP required a price of 0.00000544.
  47: - **The lock armed at two ticks.**
  48:   - 0.00000543 was 84.2815% of the way to TP, past the 70% activation level.
  49:   - That counts as one completed 10% step, so the lock was set at 50% of the TP distance: 0.000005421865, about 1.19 ticks above entry.
  50:   - The settings are confirmed twice: the runtime log shows `activationPct=70.0000`, and trade 107's stored message shows activation 70%, initial lock 40%, trail 10%.
  51: - **One tick down triggered it.** 0.00000542 was at or below the lock and above the hard floor of 0.000005412705, so the exit policy ran.
  52: - **What the exit policy saw:**
  53: 
  54:   | Timeframe | Signal | Label | Age at the trigger |
  55:   |---|---|---|---|
  56:   | 1m | 89795 | WATCH | 41.8 s |
  57:   | 5m | 89724 | WATCH | 3 m 38.8 s |
  58:   | 1h | 88327 | NEUTRAL | 58 m 37.1 s |
  59: 
  60:   The labels are stored in trade 107's message. The signal IDs are my inference from the signal times in DBQ-010 [AI, high confidence].
  61: - **Neither hold rule matched.**
  62:   - A hold needs either a 1h BUY/STRONG_BUY with a supportive 5m, or a 1h WATCH with a supportive 5m and a 1m that is not bearish.
  63:   - A NEUTRAL 1h fails both.
  64:   - The 1h was not bearish, and the 1m and 5m were not both bearish, so the rule fell through to the default `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`.
  65: - **No accepted price reached TP.** The corrected evidence chain is in objection S1.
  66: - **Classification:** a valid exit that followed the implemented rules. The decision came 94 ms after the price was observed. It was not stale data, an execution delay, a configuration problem or a logic defect.

  70: - The position closed at 23:58:43 KSA, 39 min 16 s before the 00:38 KSA candle. Nothing was open to extend.

  75: - **The lock-breach rule reads three labels and nothing else.** It does not look at distance to TP, momentum, the order book or the advisory analysis.
  76: - **A hold was impossible for the whole trade [AI from PE].**
  77:   - The deciding input was the 1h label.

  79:   - The entry signal 89469 had already recorded "1h=NEUTRAL".
  80:   - So any lock breach during this hold had to sell.
  81: - **Entry and hold use different 1h standards [AI from PE].**
  82:   - The entry was allowed under the BALANCED_STRONG profile, which accepts a bullish 5m with a NEUTRAL 1h.
  83:   - A hold after a lock breach requires the 1h to be WATCH or better.

  86:   - TP was only 2.373 ticks above entry.
  87:   - The lock armed at +2 ticks and fired at +1.
  88:   - Near-TP protection arms at 90% of the TP distance. On this grid that is the same +3 tick price as TP, and the TP branch runs first, so Near-TP protection could never arm (stored state: INACTIVE).

 140: - [Binance Developer Docs, "WebSocket Streams" (Spot)](https://developers.binance.com/docs/binance-spot-api-docs/web-socket-streams). No publication date shown; fetched this round.
 141:   - Finding: the kline stream "push[es] updates to the current klines/candlestick every second", with an update speed of "1000ms for 1s, 2000ms for the other intervals". Each update carries both the close `c` and the high `h`.
 142:   - Limitation: the page doesn't say whether a push is skipped when nothing changed. During the hold, SHIB updates arrived 2 s to 59 s apart, which suggests pushes only on change [AI].
 143:   - Relevance: protection sees the close at most once every 2 s. A price spike between updates shows up only in `h`, which protection does not use.
 144: - [Binance Fee Schedule](https://www.binance.com/en/fee/schedule) and [exchangeInfo SHIBUSDT](https://api.binance.com/api/v3/exchangeInfo?symbol=SHIBUSDT), carried over from round 1 and not re-fetched.

 148: ## OBJECTIONS
 149: **To my own round 4**
 150: - **S1. A step in my proof was wrong (the conclusion is not).**
 151:   - I argued that because the profit-lock evaluator logged nothing between 20:47:41.566Z and 20:58:09.534Z, no accepted price went above 0.00000542.
 152:   - That only rules out 0.00000543. A price at or above TP enters the TP branch and returns at `LivePositionProtectionService.java:170`, before the lock evaluator runs at `:185`, so it would never produce a lock log.
 153:   - "No accepted price reached TP" rests instead on five points:
 154:     - (a) The whole archive has zero lines matching `(TAKE_PROFIT|TP_EXTENSION).*SHIBUSDT`. Both possible TP outcomes, extending the target or selling, write log lines that include the symbol (`:120, :164, :168`).
 155:     - (b) The TP stored on managed position 64 never changed.
 156:     - (c) There is no TAKE_PROFIT trade; trade IDs 106 and 107 are consecutive.
 157:     - (d) A silent failed sell is ruled out: position quantity is checked at `:93` in the same transaction, and SELL 107 sold the full quantity with no trade in between.
 158:     - (e) The protection-failure and pending-resolved counters stayed at 0.
 159: - **S2. One claim was right but poorly supported.** I wrote "protection only ever accepted 0.00000541Г+'0.00000542Г+'0.00000543Г+'0.00000542", but before the lock armed, the logs only record new highs, not every price. It is still true, now supported by:
 160:   - the lowest 1m low from 20:45Z being 0.00000541 (DBQ-008);
 161:   - the new-high logs, which never exceed 0.00000543;
 162:   - point S1, which rules out anything at or above TP.
 163: - **S3. "The three patterns cover every rejection path" was overstated.** They cover every rejection inside price-event handling, but not messages dropped earlier in the pipeline:
 164:   - a WebSocket connection-gate rejection (`MESSAGE_REJECTED`, which doesn't name the symbol);
 165:   - a failed callback (`CALLBACK_FAILED`);
 166:   - a drain during shutdown (`KLINE_REJECTED_DRAINING`).
 167: 
 168:   Neither agent searched for these. Gate rejections are ruled out because the gate only ever moves forward (pending Г+' admitting Г+' revoked) and was still admitting SHIB messages at 20:58:00 and 20:58:43. Drain only happens at shutdown, and the counters show no restart. Failed-callback lines remain unsearched (Unknown 4).
 169: - **S4. "105 ms to the SELL" needs qualifying.** That is the timestamp set inside the transaction. The exit was logged and committed at 20:58:43.837/.839Z, 390 ms after the price was observed. Still no material delay.
 170:

In this trade, though, all three labels were the newest available for their timeframes. Any breach late in an hour will see a 1h label close to an hour old. This describes the implemented rule, not a defect in this trade.
 176: - **C5.** Codex missed the same two gaps I corrected in S1 and S3. Its conclusions are unaffected.

 179: - Query IDs DBQ-004, 005 and 006 were reused and their first results overwritten (`DATABASE_QUERY_LOG.md:62-117` vs `:271-326`).

 184: **Statistics and Replay**
 185: - One trade whose price later rose proves nothing about a better rule. Picking it because of what happened afterwards is outcome-selection bias. Any rule change would need multi-symbol, out-of-sample testing that accounts for fees, slippage and price order within each candle.
 186: - Exact-price Replay follows the live order and would read the same price events, but it skips the signal-path exits.
 187: - Fallback Replay applies target extension and lock holds to signal prices, which production's signal path does not do.
 188: - Replay was not run, so I make no parity claim.
 189: 
 190: **Entry route (outside Q1Г?"Q3; not blocking)**
 191: - **E1.** The wake-up route bought 1.1 s after 89469's own evaluation said "Wait for fresh evidence to rebuild".
 192:   - The route accepts opportunities still marked BUILDING and does not re-check that blocker.
 193:   - Its message "after the prior blocker cleared" is fixed text (`ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:446`), not a condition the code verifies.
 194:   - This is within the design, since the fresh 5m BUY counts as the new evidence, but the stored explanation overstates what was checked.
 195: - **E2.** Risk/reward was 0.877: stop 0.5% below entry, TP 0.44% above. The wake-up route has no minimum risk/reward check; other entry routes require 1.0Г?"1.25. This is a design observation, not a rule violation.

  16: /** FIX-122: evaluation-local history selection. Spring singleton state is thread-bound
  17:  * and restored in finally; open-position additions never enter this scope. Replay uses
  18:  * its own executed stops, never Production history. No wallet lock or balance writes.
  19:  */
  20: @Service
  21: @Slf4j
  22: public class StopLossEvidenceService {
  23:     private final StopLossEvidenceStore store;
  24:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replay;
  25:     private final ObjectMapper mapper;
  26:     private final boolean enabled;
  27:     private final Clock clock;
  28:     private final ThreadLocal<Context> active = new ThreadLocal<>();
  29: 
  30:     @Autowired
  31:     public StopLossEvidenceService(StopLossEvidenceStore store, ExecutionReplayScope replay,
  32:                                   ObjectMapper mapper, @Value("${trading.fix122.enabled:true}") boolean enabled) {
  33:         this(store,replay,mapper,enabled,Clock.systemUTC());
  34:     }
  35: 
  36:     StopLossEvidenceService(StopLossEvidenceStore store, ExecutionReplayScope replay,
  37:                            ObjectMapper mapper, boolean enabled, Clock clock) {
  38:         this.store=store; this.replay=replay; this.mapper=mapper; this.enabled=enabled; this.clock=clock;
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     private boolean enabled() { return replay.active() ? replay.fix122Enabled() : enabled; }
  42:     private Instant now() { return replay.active() ? replay.referenceInstant() : clock.instant(); }
  43:     private StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary resolve(String symbol, Instant at) {
  44:         return replay.active() ? replay.stopBoundary(symbol, at) : store.latest(symbol, at);
  45:     }
  46:     public boolean hasBoundary() { return active.get()!=null && active.get().boundary!=null; }
  47: 
  48:     public ExecutionDecision evaluate(TradeSignal signal, int allocation, Supplier<ExecutionDecision> action) {
  49:         if (allocation>0 || signal==null || signal.getGeneratedAt()==null || !enabled()) return action.get();
  50:         return scoped(signal, () -> new SetupWakeupEvaluation(signal, action.get())).decision();
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public SetupWakeupEvaluation wakeup(TradeSignal trigger, int allocation, Supplier<SetupWakeupEvaluation> action) {
  54:         if (allocation>0 || trigger==null || trigger.getGeneratedAt()==null || !enabled()) return action.get();
  55:         return scoped(trigger, action);
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     private SetupWakeupEvaluation scoped(TradeSignal trigger, Supplier<SetupWakeupEvaluation> action) {
  59:         Context previous=active.get();
  60:         Context c=new Context(trigger, now(), replay.active()?replay.runId():null);
  61:         active.set(c);
  62:         try {
  63:             try { c.boundary=resolve(trigger.getSymbol(), c.at); }
  64:             catch (RuntimeException error) {
  65:                 log.warn("[FIX-122] BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE symbol={} signalId={}", trigger.getSymbol(), trigger.getId(), error);
  66:                 c.status="BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE";
  67:                 ExecutionDecision d=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE",
  68:                         "Stop history could not be checked. Entry deferred to the next normal evaluation.");
  69:                 audit(c,d,"EVALUATION");
  70:                 return new SetupWakeupEvaluation(trigger,d);
  71:             }
  72:             SetupWakeupEvaluation result;
  73:             if ("1m".equals(trigger.getInterval()) && !eligible(trigger,"CURRENT_SIGNAL")) {
  74:                 result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(trigger, ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_CURRENT_CANDLE_STALE",
  75:                         "Current entry candle is not eligible after the completed stop-loss."));
  76:             } else {
  77:                 try { result=action.get(); }
  78:                 catch (StaleCurrent ignored) {
  79:                     result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(c.current,ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_CURRENT_CANDLE_STALE",
  80:                             "Wake-up timing candle is not eligible after the completed stop-loss."));
  81:                 }
  82:             }
  83:             if (result!=null && result.decision()!=null) {
  84:                 result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(result.executionSignal(), result.decision()
  85:                         .withStopBoundary(new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Stamp(c.boundary)));
  86:             }
  87:             audit(c,result==null?null:result.decision(),"EVALUATION");
  88:             return result==null?SetupWakeupEvaluation.none():result;
  89:         } finally {
  90:             if(previous==null) active.remove(); else active.set(previous);
  91:         }
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     public void requireCurrent(TradeSignal signal) {
  95:         if(active.get()!=null) active.get().current=signal;
  96:         if(!eligible(signal,"CURRENT_SIGNAL")) throw new StaleCurrent();
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     public boolean eligible(TradeSignal signal, String purpose) {
 100:         Context c=active.get();
 101:         if(c==null || c.boundary==null) return true;
 102:         String reason=signal==null?"LINEAGE_UNRESOLVED":StopLossEvidencePolicy.exclusion(
 103:                 signal.getInterval(),signal.getCandleOpenTime(),signal.getGeneratedAt(),c.at,c.boundary);
 104:         if(reason==null) return true;
 105:         Map<String,Object> item=new LinkedHashMap<>();
 106:         item.put("purpose",purpose); item.put("reason",reason);
 107:         if(signal!=null) {
 108:             item.put("signalId",signal.getId()); item.put("interval",signal.getInterval());
 109:             item.put("candleOpenTime",String.valueOf(signal.getCandleOpenTime()));
 110:             item.put("decision",String.valueOf(signal.getDecision()));
 111:         }
 112:         if(!c.excluded.contains(item)) c.excluded.add(item);
 113:         return false;
 114:     }
 115: 
 116:     /** Read again at execution-price approval. A newly committed stop invalidates the
 117:      * old approval. This is not a repair of the separate wallet transaction race.
 118:      */
 119:     public ExecutionDecision recheck(TradeSignal signal, ExecutionDecision approved) {
 120:         if(approved==null || !approved.allowed() || approved.stopBoundary()==null || !enabled()) return approved;
 121:         Context c=new Context(signal,now(),replay.active()?replay.runId():null);
 122:         ExecutionDecision result=approved;
 123:         try {
 124:             c.boundary=resolve(signal.getSymbol(),c.at);
 125:             if(!Objects.equals(approved.stopBoundary().boundary(),c.boundary)) {
 126:                 c.status="BOUNDARY_CHANGED";
 127:                 result=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_CHANGED",
 128:                         "A stop-loss boundary changed after qualification. Wait for a fresh evaluation.", approved.evidence());
 129:             }
 130:         } catch(RuntimeException error) {
 131:             c.status="BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE";
 132:             result=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE",
 133:                     "Stop history unavailable at price revalidation. Entry deferred.",approved.evidence());
 134:             log.warn("[FIX-122] RECHECK_UNAVAILABLE symbol={}",signal.getSymbol(),error);
 135:         }
 136:         audit(c,result,"PRICE_RECHECK");
 137:         return result;
 138:     }
 139: 
 140:     private void audit(Context c,ExecutionDecision d,String stage) {
 141:         Map<String,Object> p=new LinkedHashMap<>();
 142:         p.put("revision","FIX-122"); p.put("source",c.runId==null?"PRODUCTION":"REPLAY");
 143:         p.put("status",c.status!=null?c.status:c.boundary==null?"NOT_APPLICABLE":
 144:                 c.excluded.isEmpty()?"APPLIED_NO_EXCLUSIONS":"APPLIED_EXCLUSIONS");
 145:         p.put("signalId",c.current.getId()); p.put("candleOpenTime",String.valueOf(c.current.getCandleOpenTime()));
 146:         p.put("triggerSignalId",c.trigger.getId());
 147:         p.put("stopExecutionId",c.boundary==null?null:c.boundary.executionId());
 148:         p.put("stopAt",c.boundary==null?null:c.boundary.executedAt().toString());
 149:         p.put("excluded",c.excluded);
 150:         p.put("allowed",d!=null&&d.allowed()); p.put("route",d==null?null:d.source());
 151:         p.put("code",d==null?"NO_WAKEUP":d.code()); p.put("explanation",d==null?null:d.explanation());
 152:         if(d!=null && d.evidence()!=null) {
 153:             p.put("buyCount",d.evidence().buyCount()); p.put("watchCount",d.evidence().watchCount());
 154:             p.put("evidenceScore",d.evidence().evidenceScore()); p.put("health",d.evidence().opportunityHealth());
 155:             p.put("positionPercent",d.positionPercent());
 156:         }
 157:         try { store.append(c.runId,c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),c.at,stage,mapper.writeValueAsString(p)); }
 158:         catch(Exception error) { log.error("[FIX-122] DIAGNOSTIC_PERSIST_FAILED symbol={} signalId={}",
 159:                 c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),error); }
 160:         if(c.boundary!=null || c.status!=null) log.info(
 161:                 "[FIX-122] {} symbol={} signalId={} stop={} excluded={} code={} allowed={}",stage,
 162:                 c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),c.boundary,c.excluded.size(),p.get("code"),p.get("allowed"));
 163:     }

 169:         final List<Map<String,Object>> excluded=new ArrayList<>();
 170:         Context(TradeSignal trigger,Instant at,Long runId) { this.trigger=trigger;this.current=trigger;this.at=at;this.runId=runId; }
 171:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceStore.java lines 1-100 =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   5: import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
   6: import org.springframework.transaction.TransactionDefinition;
   7: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
   8: import javax.sql.DataSource;
   9: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  10: import java.time.Instant;
  11: import java.util.List;
  12: import java.util.Map;
  13: 
  14: /** FIX-122: bounded, non-locking committed ledger reads; no managed-position row locks.
  15:  * STOP_LOSS is the terminal reason written by executeSignalLinkedExit. The distinct
  16:  * POSITION_STOP_LOSS and partial-harvest reasons are deliberately not included.
  17:  */
  18: @Service
  19: public class StopLossEvidenceStore {
  20:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  21:     private final TransactionTemplate read;
  22:     private final TransactionTemplate write;
  23: 
  24:     public StopLossEvidenceStore(DataSource dataSource, PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
  25:         jdbc = new JdbcTemplate(dataSource);
  26:         jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
  27:         read = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  28:         read.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  29:         read.setIsolationLevel(TransactionDefinition.ISOLATION_READ_COMMITTED);
  30:         read.setReadOnly(true);
  31:         read.setTimeout(2);
  32:         write = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  33:         write.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  34:         write.setTimeout(2);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary latest(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  38:         return read.execute(status -> jdbc.query("""
  39:                 SELECT id, executed_at FROM wallet_trade
  40:                 WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED' AND side='SELL'
  41:                   AND execution_reason='STOP_LOSS' AND executed_at<=?
  42:                 ORDER BY executed_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1
  43:                 """, (rs, row) -> new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(rs.getLong(1),
  44:                 rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()), symbol, Timestamp.from(asOf))
  45:                 .stream().findFirst().orElse(null));
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     public void append(Long runId, String symbol, Long signalId, Instant at, String stage, String payload) {
  49:         // Separate audit transaction survives a caller rollback; it records an evaluation,
  50:         // never asserts that a BUY/SELL actually executed. No FK to wallet rows is acquired.
  51:         write.executeWithoutResult(status -> jdbc.update("""
  52:                 INSERT INTO fix122_evaluation(test_run_id,symbol,signal_id,evaluated_at,stage,payload)
  53:                 VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)
  54:                 """, runId, symbol, signalId, Timestamp.from(at), stage, payload));
  55:     }
  56: 
  57:     public List<Map<String,Object>> replayDetails(long runId) {
  58:         return jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id=? ORDER BY id", runId);
  59:     }
  60: 
  61:     public List<Map<String,Object>> productionDetails(String symbol, Instant from, Instant to) {
  62:         return jdbc.queryForList("""
  63:                 SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id IS NULL AND symbol=?
  64:                 AND evaluated_at>=? AND evaluated_at<=? ORDER BY id
  65:                 """, symbol, Timestamp.from(from), Timestamp.from(to));
  66:     }
  67: }

 137:     // FIX-140: classify only known lock cancellation/deadlock errors. Communications
 138:     // errors are deliberately excluded even when nested below a data-access exception.
 139:     static boolean retryableSymbolLock(Throwable failure) {
 140:         for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())
 141:             if(e instanceof java.sql.SQLException sql && sql.getSQLState()!=null && sql.getSQLState().startsWith("08"))return false;
 142:         for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())

===== codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md lines 1-25 =====
   1: # Crypto AI Trader ƒ?" Architecture
   2: 
   3: Status labels used in this document:
   4: 
   5: - **Current** ƒ?" present in the repository today.
   6: - **Pending** ƒ?" approved design direction, not implemented or deployed.
   7: - **Legacy/shared** ƒ?" existing shared-market path retained as historical evidence
   8:   while the trader moves to local-candle ownership.
   9: 
  10: ## Current release status
  11: 
  12: **FIX-153 is implemented in source but is NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION.**
  13: It moves the trader toward collector-free, trader-owned local candle ingestion
  14: in `shared-market.mode=OFF`. Java 21, disposable-MySQL, crash/race, Windows
  15: two-process, soak, OFF-mode and Replay-parity proof gates remain open.
  16: 
  17: FIX-154 is a separate pending higher-timeframe design. It is not implemented
  18: and is not part of FIX-153 deployment scope.
  19: 
  20: ## System purpose
  21: 
  22: The application is a Spring Boot cryptocurrency trader that analyses Binance
  23: market data, persists candles and technical indicators, creates trade signals,
  24: and performs paper-wallet execution and position protection. Production and
  25: Replay are intended to use equivalent persisted market evidence.

===== codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md lines 160-210 =====
 160: 
 161: ```text
 162: PENDING
 163:   -> APPLIED                 (protection effects and state commit together)
 164:   -> NON_CANONICAL           (invalid, stale, out-of-order, or duplicate)
 165:   -> PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED  (confirmed rollback/crash resolution)
 166: ```
 167: 
 168: Replay and price authority consume only accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` rows.
 169: Replay must reject a parity claim when the requested range contains unresolved
 170: direct `PENDING` evidence; this must not block live trading.
 171: 
 172: The automatic resolver is valid only for the paper-trading phase, where every
 173: protection effect commits in the same transaction as its `APPLIED` update.
 174: Before deployment, the code must prove that there are no asynchronous,
 175: `REQUIRES_NEW`, in-memory-only, or external effects in this boundary. A future
 176: live-exchange order needs a separate idempotent/exchange-reconciliation design.
 177: 
 178: The target runtime also requires exactly one active trader process. This is an
 179: operational prerequisite, not a database lease or fence.
 180: 
 181: ## Pending: FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY validation
 182: 
 183: FIX-154 is separate from the local-candle change and is not implemented.
 184: 
 185: Its purpose is to let a historical bearish 1h signal affect a current 1m BUY
 186: only when the historical evidence is fresh, quality-valid, and sufficiently
 187: strong. `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` on a historical signal is investigatory
 188: evidence, not a higher-timeframe quality gate; the current 1m BTC protection
 189: remains unchanged.
 190: 
 191: The design includes reliability bands (`NOT_TRUSTED`, `SOFT_CONFLICT`,
 192: `HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE`), required fresh/quality-valid bearish 5m support for
 193: a hard veto, persisted `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`, recovery release
 194: rules, immutable outcome audit evidence, and Replay/no-look-ahead parity.
 195: 
 196: The implementation design is maintained in `codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md`.
 197: It supersedes no existing deployed behavior and is deliberately separate from
 198: the unrelated legacy readiness document under `src/main/resources/md/FIX-146.md`.
 199: 
 200: ## Deployment posture
 201: 
 202: Neither FIX-153 nor FIX-154 is deployment-ready. FIX-153 source hardening is
 203: implemented but unverified; FIX-154 remains pending.
 204: 
 205: Required validation includes Java 21 build/tests, disposable MySQL bootstrap,
 206: real-MySQL concurrency and lock-wait tests, crash/restart tests, stale-input
 207: tests, Replay parity checks, OFF-mode health/admin checks, collector-free
 208: runtime verification, deterministic dispatcher/detector/drain tests,
 209: two-process Windows lock rehearsal, and sustained-load observation across
 210: enabled symbols and intervals. This local workspace has Java 17 and cannot

===== codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md lines 245-285 =====
 245: of the WebSocket or protection path and is not a persisted trading fence.
 246: 
 247: ### Outcome-schema startup invariant
 248: 
 249: The active signal-processing runtime writes `candle_analysis_outcome` for
 250: terminal analysis evidence. V108 therefore ships with the FIX-154 release even
 251: though local-candle reconciliation remains disabled. `CandleAnalysisOutcomeSchemaVerifier`
 252: runs after Flyway and fails application startup with a clear message if the
 253: table is absent; this prevents later processing transactions from failing only
 254: when they attempt their first outcome write.
 255: # FIX-154 implementation update ƒ?" 2026-10-10
 256: 
 257: `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService` is the single pure assessment boundary for
 258: the 5m/1h BUY context. It returns effective decisions separately from the JPA
 259: entities, so a SOFT 1h conflict can be assessed as effective `NEUTRAL` without
 260: ever changing the persisted signal. `TradeExecutionValidationService` uses it
 261: for direct BUY and accumulated-evidence authority; `OpportunityConsolidationService`
 262: uses the same assessment for consolidation. A trusted SOFT bearish 1h limits an
 263: initial entry to 25%; a trusted HARD or unknown bearish 1h rejects it, while
 264: the legacy 5m bearish veto keeps priority.
 265: 
 266: `buy_htf_assessment_audit` is append-only operational evidence. Its writer is
 267: best-effort and uses a bounded dedicated executor: auditing never borrows an
 268: execution transaction's database connection, is skipped in Replay, and cannot
 269: be part of a BUY decision. The progressive add route retains its
 270: existing raw-1h guard; it writes `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` evidence only after
 271: that guard has already rejected an add.
 272: 
 273: `GET /api/fix154/health` exposes non-gating counters for untrusted bearish
 274: blocks, soft caps, soft add blocks, and best-effort audit failures.
 275: 
 276: ## FIX-153 automatic local-candle reconciliation ƒ?" enabled 2026-10-10
 277: 
 278: In `shared-market.mode=OFF`, `LocalCandleReconciliationService` runs every
 279: five seconds on its dedicated single-worker executor. It finds eligible
 280: missing or interrupted candle identities inside the data-quality window, reads
 281: the authoritative Binance candle, and writes only verified final evidence.
 282: An existing open row is finalized by an update-only path; a missing row uses a
 283: plain insert-if-absent backfill path. Neither route creates a close token,
 284: dispatches analysis, or executes a trade. The next genuine live close resumes
 285: ordinary analysis after the quality window is complete.

codex
Source verification confirms two important corrections and several new requirements: the Binance minimum-notional call is currently latent because Replay-generated signals have no IDs, but that same null-ID defect disables near-TP harvest and BUY-continuation logic; and the working tree’s FIX-154 code/migrations contradict the draft’s “out of scope” statement. I’m consolidating these into a single requirements list with only source-supported claims.

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response correctly preserves the prior rejection: it says RPR-0.4 is neither amended nor approved and requires a complete replacement specification. [Response](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/RPR-0.4_REVIEW_RESPONSE.md:9)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** CR-01, CR-02, CR-04, CR-07, CR-08, and CR-09 are directionally correct: artifact-level registry, shared-class Production impact, tiered parity claims, frozen source/migration reconciliation, deterministic scope, and user-approved retirements are all necessary. They are not sufficient requirements yet. [Response](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/RPR-0.4_REVIEW_RESPONSE.md:18)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response underhandles the pre-write problem. Replay writes generated-signal output before its market-price evidence assertion. The next specification must require qualification before any generated signals, shadow-wallet rows, management rows, or execution records are written—apart from a narrowly defined refusal audit. [Worker writes](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:174) [price guard](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:195)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Replay-generated signals have null IDs. `AnalysisService.analyzeForRegression` returns an unsaved entity; the Replay persistence deliberately excludes `id`; ID-dependent near-TP and BUY-consumption rules therefore do not behave like Production. - **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response identifies some orchestration gaps but omits material path differences. Production’s signal path evaluates TP, label-free profit-lock trigger, stop-loss, rebase floor, SELL suppression, then progressive adds. Replay fallback evaluates stop-loss before profit lock, applies a label-dependent lock decision, and runs continuation logic at TP. The specification must define each distinct price-event and signal-price path, rather than assert a single shared order. ## EVIDENCE

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The currently available comparator is pre-wallet, signal-only, and has no production caller. It is not an entry/lifecycle oracle. [Validator](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java:10)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Potential read-only decision evidence exists, but it is incomplete by design. A window with no starting position can still be ineligible for lifecycle/entry parity. ## OBJECTIONS

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The next single complete specification must include these registered requirements:

  1. Freeze an exact, reproducible source baseline: commit plus the complete approved dirty diff; declare actual migration inventory, Flyway history assumptions, FIX-154 source state, and deployment-status unknowns.
  2. Define three table classes: prohibited account state; immutable replay profile/configuration; and permitted read-only decision/oracle evidence. State whether wallet-derived fields embedded in evidence are usable.
  3. Interpose every Replay decision-time configuration read—not merely minimum notional—including `TradeExecutionValidationService` settings.
  4. Require parity preflight before all business-output writes, with an exhaustive side-effect inventory and a test proving refusal leaves no generated-signal or shadow-wallet output.
  5. Give every regenerated signal a deterministic, non-null, collision-safe Replay identity respected by all ID-based policies; mark pre-change runs non-parity for near-TP and BUY-continuation coverage.
  6. Define both Replay price-event and signal-price fallback state machines against the corresponding Production path, including advisory `POSITION_STOP_LOSS`, TP behavior, lock behavior, stop precedence, SELL suppression, adds, and stop-boundary effects.
  7. Enforce deny-by-default Replay I/O: no live HTTP, wallet reads, Production mutations, or nonessential shared side effects. Test this with a reachable near-TP harvest, not a vacuous current path.
  8. Define immutable price-event identity, ordering-slot, close-message lineage, accepted-stream coverage, tie handling, and ambiguity refusal.
  9. Define tier-specific comparators and oracles. Match signals by candle identity, report `generated_at` as clock evidence rather than equality, and specify evidence completeness/refusal rules for FIX-122 and processing records.
  10. Define no-look-ahead using both as-of and availability/commit time; refuse when same-boundary order cannot be proven. Do not equate Java-set `created_at` with commit time.
  11. Define starting-state eligibility including the 120-minute opportunity-anchor horizon, or authorize a bounded read-only seed from decision evidence.
  12. Resolve late/retry signal-lookup semantics, historical context injection/refusal, and the intentionally unavailable/lossy derivatives and order-book cases.
  13. Choose single-symbol-first or a fully deterministic multi-symbol scheduler; prove Flyway baseline behavior on disposable MySQL with the bundled Flyway version. The existing baseline test only resolves migration names, not MySQL migration execution. [Baseline test](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/test/java/com/crypto/local/BaselineResolutionTest.java:8)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response’s `RegimeStateService` reset is not a material parity requirement without a demonstrated active state path in `PRODUCTION_PARITY`; it should not displace the omissions above. [Replay default](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:27)

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** I did not access Production, databases, runtime logs, deployment systems, or execute tests.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The deployed JAR/configuration, actual Flyway history, whether FIX-154 is deployed, and completeness of FIX-122/processing evidence are unverified.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Strict wallet/position isolation can support analysis parity and limited no-open-position entry-decision comparison only where a complete oracle is demonstrated; it cannot establish lifecycle parity from the current evidence by assumption.
